BUSINESS REVIEW FOR THE REPORTING                                       報告期業務回顧
PERIOD
We are a global value retailer offering a variety of trendy lifestyle   我們是一家提供以IP設計為特色的豐富多樣的潮流
products featuring IP design. Since we opened our first store           生活產品的全球價值零售商。自2013年在中國開設
in China in 2013, we have successfully incubated two brands             第一家門店以來，我們已成功孵化了兩個品牌，分
– “MINISO” and “TOP TOY”. We have built our flagship brand              別是「名創優品」和「TOP TOY」。我們已將旗艦品
“MINISO” as a globally recognized retail brand and established          牌「名創優品」打造為全球認可的零售品牌，並在全
a store network worldwide. Our flagship brand “MINISO” offers           球範圍內建立了門店網絡。我們的旗艦品牌「名創優
a frequently-refreshed assortment of lifestyle products covering        品」提供種類繁多及高頻上新的生活家居產品，可滿
diverse consumer needs, and consumers are attracted to our              足消費者多樣化的需求，以時尚、創意、高品質及
products’ trendiness, creativeness, high quality and affordability.     高性價比的產品吸引消費者。


During the six months ended June 30, 2025, the total number of          於截至2025年6月30日止六個月，本集團於中國內
MINISO stores in mainland China and overseas markets increased          地和海外市場的名創優品門店總數由截至2024 年
from 7,504 as of December 31, 2024 to 7,612 as of June 30,              12月31日的7,504家增加至截至2025年6月30日的
2025. The number of TOP TOY stores increased from 276 as of             7,612家。TOP TOY門店數量由截至2024年12月31
December 31, 2024 to 293 as of June 30, 2025. For the six months        日的276家增加至截至2025年6月30日的293家。截
ended June 30, 2025, the aggregate GMV of the Group reached             至2025年6月30日止六個月，本集團產生的GMV總
approximately RMB16.7 billion.                                          計約人民幣167億元。


Brands and Products                                                     品牌及產品
For the six months ended June 30, 2025, we launched an average          截至2025年6月30日止六個月，我們在「名創優品」
of around 1,800 SKUs in “MINISO” channels per month, and we             各渠道平均每月推出約1,800個SKU，為消費者提

Foreign Exchange Risk                                                    匯兌風險
Our financial reporting currency is RMB and changes in foreign           我們的財務報告貨幣為人民幣，匯率變動會顯著影
exchange rates can significantly affect our reported results and         響我們報告的業績及綜合趨勢。此外，我們的經營
consolidated trends. In addition, our results of operations, including   業績（包括利潤率）受到匯率波動的影響。我們的國
margins, are affected by the fluctuation in foreign exchange rates.      際業務主要以美元產生收入。一般而言，人民幣兌
Our international operations generate revenues primarily in U.S.         美元貶值對我們的經營業績產生積極影響，而人民
dollars. Generally, a weakening of RMB against U.S. dollar has a         幣兌美元升值則相反。我們並未使用任何衍生金融
positive effect on our results of operations, while a strengthening of   工具對沖有關風險。若我們需要將美元兌換成人民
RMB against U.S. dollar has the opposite effect. We have not used        幣用於營運，人民幣兌美元升值將對我們從兌換中
any derivative financial instruments to hedge exposure to such risk.     獲得的人民幣金額產生不利影響。相反，若我們決
To the extent that we need to convert U.S. dollars into RMB for our      定將人民幣兌換為美元用於支付普通股或美國存託
operations, appreciation of RMB against U.S. dollar would have an        股股息或其他業務目的，則美元兌人民幣升值將對
adverse effect on RMB amount we receive from the conversion.             我們獲得的美元金額產生不利影響。
Conversely, if we decide to convert RMB into U.S. dollars for the
purpose of making payments for dividends on our ordinary shares
or ADSs or for other business purposes, appreciation of U.S. dollar
against RMB would have a negative effect on U.S. dollar amounts
available to us.

2020 SHARE INCENTIVE PLAN                                              2020年股份激勵計劃
The 2020 Share Incentive Plan was first adopted by the Board           2020年股份激勵計劃於2020年9月首次獲董事會採
in September 2020 and was further amended in June 2022, the            納，並於2022年6月進一步修訂，修訂於上市後生
amendments of which took effect upon the Listing. Since the            效。自上市起，2020年股份激勵計劃受《上市規則》
Listing, the 2020 Share Incentive Plan was subject to Chapter          第17章規限。本公司倚賴《上市規則》新第17章（自
17 of the Listing Rules. The Company relies on the transitional        2023年1月1日起生效）所規定的過渡安排。
arrangements provided for under the new Chapter 17 of the Listing
Rules which became effective on January 1, 2023.

Maximum number of Shares                                               最大股份數目
The maximum aggregate number of Shares that may be issued is           可發行的股份最高總數為147,301,128股股份（「計
147,301,128 Shares (the “Scheme Mandate”) of which only up to          劃授權」），其中僅有最多71,485,122股股份可根據

the form of Options.

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                         審閱範圍
We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong Standard           我們已根據香港會計師公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒
on Review Engagements 2410 Review of Interim Financial                  佈的《香港審閱準則第2410號 － 實體的獨立核數師
Information Performed by the Independent Auditor of the Entity as       對中期財務資料的審閱》進行審閱。中期財務報告審
issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants       閱工作包括主要向負責財務會計事項的人員作出查
(“HKICPA”). A review of interim financial report consists of            詢，並執行分析和其他審閱程序。由於審閱的範圍
making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial        遠較按照《香港審計準則》進行的審計範圍為小，所
and accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review        以不能保證我們會注意到在審計中可能會被發現的
procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit       所有重大事項。因此，我們不會發表任何審計意見。
conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing
and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that
we would become aware of all significant matters that might be
identified in an audit. Accordingly, we do not express an audit
opinion.

1      BASIS OF PREPARATION                                               1   編製基準
      This interim financial report for the six months ended June 30,         截至2025年6月30日止六個月，本中期財務
      2025 has been prepared in accordance with the applicable                報告乃根據《香港聯合交易所有限公司（「香港
      disclosure provisions of the Rules Governing the Listing of             聯交所」）證券上市規則》之適用披露規定，包
      Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the              括遵守由國際會計準則理事會（「國際會計準則
      “Hong Kong Stock Exchange”), including compliance with                  理事會」）頒佈之《國際會計準則》 （「國際會計
      International Accounting Standard (“IAS”) 34, Interim financial         準則」）第34號「中期財務報告」而編製。其於
      reporting , issued by the International Accounting Standards            2025年8月21日獲授權刊發。
      Board (“IASB”). It was authorized for issue on August 21,

4   REVENUE                                                           4      收入
    The Group’s revenue is primarily derived from the sale of                本集團的收入主要來自通過中國和海外的直營
    lifestyle and pop toy products through self-operated stores,             店、加盟店、線下代理商銷售生活家居產品和
    franchised stores, offline distributors in the PRC and overseas          潮流玩具產品，以及通過本集團在第三方電商
    and online sales conducted through the Group’s self-operated             平台上的自營網店以及通過線上代理商進行的
    online stores on third-party e-commerce platforms and                    線上銷售。其他收入來源主要包括來自加盟商
    through online distributors. Other sources of revenue mainly             和代理商的授權費、基於銷售的特許權使用費
    include license fees, sales-based royalties and sales-based              及基於銷售的管理及諮詢服務費。
    management and consultation service fees from franchisees

             The following table presents the fair value of the Group’s              下表呈列於期末本集團按經常性基準計

             Fair value measurement .

              The level into which a fair value measurement is classified            公允價值計量所歸類的層級乃經參考以
              is determined with reference to the observability and                  下估值方法所用輸入數據的可觀察性及
              significance of the inputs used in the valuation technique             重要性而釐定：
              as follows:

              •      Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using only              •   第一層級估值：僅使用第一層級
                     Level 1 inputs i.e., unadjusted quoted prices in                    輸入數據（即於計量日期的相同資
                     active markets for identical assets or liabilities at               產或負債於活躍市場的未經調整報
                     the measurement date.                                               價）計量的公允價值。

              •      Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using Level             •   第二層級估值：使用第二層級輸入
                     2 inputs i.e., observable inputs which fail to meet                 數據（即未能達致第一層級的可觀
                     Level 1 and do not use significant unobservable                     察輸入數據）且並非使用重大不可
                     inputs. Unobservable inputs are inputs for which                    觀察輸入數據計量的公允價值。不
                     market data are not available.                                      可觀察輸入數據為無市場數據的輸
                                                                                         入數據。

                                       of merchandises sold by us, our MINISO Retail Partner and distributors operating

                                       值，以及我們向非運營TOP TOY門店的代理商銷售的商品價值的總額。兩者均扣除銷
                                       售回扣前並包括向消費者收取的增值稅和銷售稅（如適用），無論商品是否退回
